Title: Compendio de la prodigiosa vida del venerable siervo de Dios Fray Juan Massías, religioso lego del Orden de N. P. Santo Domingo
Creator: Alonso de Toro, O.P.
Publisher: Sevilla, 1689 (the printer's name is garbled in the scan)
Publication Date: 1689
Rights: Public Domain (published 1689, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: spa
Source: Internet Archive item A024187 (https://archive.org/details/A024187), the item's own A024187_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Biblioteca de la Universidad de Sevilla; host metadata date: 1689. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 5ee662bff2da07172f2e6472d5cc5f1960bedbf2f8e27dba31f1e846eefd913c.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Biblioteca de la Universidad de Sevilla scan; the title page is garbled, the body usable with the long s read as f.
Content note: A short Life of Juan Macías, the Dominican lay brother and porter of the convent of the Magdalena in Lima, companion in sanctity to Martín de Porres: his charity to the poor at the gate, his prayer before the crucifix of the porter's chapel, his penances and miracles.
